from the system of equations (
28
) and (
G.2
), we obtain the method-of-moments estimator

\displaystyle{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}=\frac{(n+p+1)}{n(n+1)}\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Y}-\frac{1}{n(n+1)}\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Y},

and
{\mathbb{E}}[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]=\boldsymbol{S}
.

Now we derive the variance lower bound for
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}
. Since
{\mathbb{E}}[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]=\boldsymbol{S}
,
{\mathbb{E}}\big[\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big]=\sum_{t,t^{\prime}}\text{Var}\big\{[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}\big\}.
By definition of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}
,

\displaystyle[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}=\frac{n+p+1}{n(n+1)}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}-\frac{1}{n(n+1)}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}.

Since
\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}=\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)}+{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}}^{(t)},\quad\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}=\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})}+{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}}^{(t^{\prime})},
for
t\neq t^{\prime}
,
without loss of generality, we assume
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)}=a_{0}\boldsymbol{e}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})}=a_{1}\boldsymbol{e}_{1}+a_{2}\boldsymbol{e}_{2}
for some constants
a_{0},a_{1},a_{2}
.
If necessary, we could let
\boldsymbol{u}_{1}=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)}/\lVert\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)}\rVert
, and
\boldsymbol{u}_{2}={\widetilde{\boldsymbol{u}}}_{2}/\lVert{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{u}}}_{2}\rVert
where
{\widetilde{\boldsymbol{u}}}_{2}=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})}-\boldsymbol{P}_{\boldsymbol{u}_{1}}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})}
, and completing the basis to obtain an orthonormal basis
\{\boldsymbol{u}_{1},\boldsymbol{u}_{2},\ldots,\boldsymbol{u}_{p}\}
for
{\mathbb{R}}^{p}
. Let
\boldsymbol{U}=[\boldsymbol{u}_{1},\boldsymbol{u}_{2},\ldots,\boldsymbol{u}_{p}]
, then
\boldsymbol{U}
is an orthogonal matrix, hence
\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{U}
and
\boldsymbol{X}
have the same distribution, only the first coordinate of
\boldsymbol{U}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)}
is nonzero, and only the first two coordinates of
\boldsymbol{U}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})}
are be nonzero. That is, we could perform change of variables by replacing
(\boldsymbol{X},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})})
with
(\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{U},\boldsymbol{U}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{U}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\beta}^{(t^{\prime})})
.

Therefore,
\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}
and
\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}
are independent of
\{\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}:3\leq j\leq p\}
.
Let
{\cal F}=\sigma(\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})},\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{1},\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{2})
be the
\sigma-
field generated by
(\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)},\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})},\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{1},\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{2})
, then

\displaystyle\text{Var}\big\{[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}\big\}

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}\big[\text{Var}\big\{[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}|{\cal F}\big\}\big]=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\big[\text{Var}\big\{[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}|{\cal F}\big\}\big].

Note that in the above display,

\displaystyle[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}=\sum_{j=1}^{2}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}+\sum_{j=3}^{p}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})},

where the first term is measurable with respect to
{\cal F}
, and the second term is a quadratic form

\displaystyle\sum_{j=3}^{p}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}=\sum_{j=3}^{p}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}=\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\boldsymbol{\xi},

here
\boldsymbol{\xi}=[\boldsymbol{e}_{3}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top},\ldots,\boldsymbol{e}_{p}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}]^{\top}\sim{\cal N}(\boldsymbol{0},\boldsymbol{I}_{n(p-2)})
, and
\boldsymbol{\Lambda}=\boldsymbol{I}_{p-2}\otimes\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}[\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}]^{\top}
.
Thus, for
t\neq t^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\text{Var}\big\{[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}\big\}\geq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\Big\{\text{Var}\big\{\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\boldsymbol{\xi}|{\cal F}\big\}\Big\}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\Big\{\lVert\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle\geq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{\Lambda}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{p-2}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}\rVert^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}].

For
t=t^{\prime}
, using a similar argument we obtain

\displaystyle\text{Var}\big\{[{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}]_{t,t^{\prime}}\big\}\geq~\frac{p-1}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}\rVert^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}].

Summing over all
(t,t^{\prime})\in[T]\times[T]
yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\big[\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}_{\rm{(mm)}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\big]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{p-2}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}\sum_{t,t^{\prime}}{\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t)}\rVert^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{y}^{(t^{\prime})}\rVert^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{p-2}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{4}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{p-2}{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}}({\mathbb{E}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}])^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{p-2}{(n+1)^{2}}[\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\rVert^{2}]^{2}.

\blacksquare

G.3
Proof of Theorem
3.3

Proof of
Theorem

3.3
.

Recall definition of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}
in
Definition

2.2
, and let
\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}
,
\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}
be defined as in
Propositions

F.7
and
F.8
. With
\boldsymbol{Z}=\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1/2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle n^{2}\Big[\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)n^{-\frac{1}{2}}-n^{-1}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\big(\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\big(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}(p\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})+(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\big)

\displaystyle-\big[(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{S}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}))\big]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\big(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}(p\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\big)-(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{S}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}))

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}+\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-(n+p)\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{S}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{S}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})+\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}+\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}((n+p)\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top})\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{S}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})-(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}){\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})(\boldsymbol{S}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}})(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}).

Therefore, by triangle inequality and
\left\lVert\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
in
Lemmas

C.1
and
C.2
,

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)(\boldsymbol{S}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\big\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{1}{2}\big[\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)\big]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}})n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})).

Therefore,

\big\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)(\boldsymbol{S}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\big\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\Theta_{1}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})),

where
\Theta_{1}=\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
.
Note that we have
{\mathbb{E}}[\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]\leq 4
from
Proposition

F.7
.
By
Proposition

F.8
, we have

(1)

under
4
(i),
{\mathbb{E}}[\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]\leq C(\tau^{\prime})(T\wedge(1+\frac{p}{n}))(1+\frac{p}{n})
.
Hence

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Theta_{1}^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}[\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2[4+C(\tau^{\prime})(T\wedge(1+\frac{p}{n}))(1+\frac{p}{n})]

\displaystyle\leq C(\tau^{\prime})(T\wedge(1+\frac{p}{n}))(1+\frac{p}{n}).

(2)

under
4
(ii),
{\mathbb{E}}[I(\Omega)\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]\leq C(\gamma,c)
with
{\mathbb{P}}(\Omega)\to 1
. Thus,

{\mathbb{E}}[I(\Omega)\Theta_{1}^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}[\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+I(\Omega)\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]\leq C(\gamma,c)
.

\blacksquare

G.4
Proof of Theorem
3.4

Proof of
Theorem

3.4
.

From the definitions of
\boldsymbol{Q}_{1},\boldsymbol{Q}_{2},\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}
in
Propositions

F.7
,
F.8
and
F.9
, we have

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}^{\top}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{3})n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)+(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}+\boldsymbol{S})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\big[n^{-1}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}-(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}+\boldsymbol{S})\big](\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)({\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n),

where
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}n^{-1}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}
, and
\boldsymbol{R}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}+\boldsymbol{S}
.

Therefore, by triangle inequality and
\left\lVert\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
from
Lemmas

C.1
and
C.2
,

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)({\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\big\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}})n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\big(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}})n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S}))

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\Theta_{2}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})),

where
\Theta_{2}=\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
.
By
Propositions

F.7
,
F.8
and
F.9
, we obtain

{\mathbb{E}}[\Theta_{2}]\leq C(\gamma,\tau^{\prime})
under
4
(i)
and

{\mathbb{E}}[I(\Omega)\Theta_{2}]\leq C(\gamma,c)
with
P(\Omega)\to 1
under
4
(ii).

Furthermore, since
\Theta_{2}=O_{P}(1)
, and
\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}=O_{P}(1)
from
Lemmas

C.1
and
C.2
,

\displaystyle\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\Theta_{2}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S}))

\displaystyle=O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})).

Since
\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}=(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n){\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)
, taking trace of both sides gives
\frac{1}{n}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\rVert_{*}
thanks to
\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
. Note that
\lVert\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{*}=\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})
by definition of
\boldsymbol{R}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})(\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\rVert_{*}+\lVert\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{*}).

(30)

Since
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}
and
\boldsymbol{R}
are both
T\times T
positive semi-definite matrices, whose ranks are at most
T
,

\displaystyle\big|\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\|_{*}-\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{*}\big|\leq\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R}\|_{*}\leq\sqrt{2T}\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}-\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle O_{P}((T/n)^{\frac{1}{2}})(\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\rVert_{*}+\lVert\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{*})=o_{P}(1)(\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\rVert_{*}+\lVert\boldsymbol{R}\rVert_{*}),

thanks to
T=o(n)
. That is,

\displaystyle\frac{\big|\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\|_{*}-\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{*}\big|}{\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\|_{*}+\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{*}}\leq O_{P}((T/n)^{\frac{1}{2}}),

which implies

\frac{\|\boldsymbol{R}\|_{*}}{\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{R}}}\|_{*}}-1=O_{P}((T/n)^{\frac{1}{2}})
, i.e.,

\frac{\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n}-1=O_{P}((T/n)^{\frac{1}{2}})=o_{P}(1).

\blacksquare

G.5
Proof of Theorem
3.5

Proof of
Theorem

3.5
.

This proof is based on results of
Theorems

3.3
and
3.4
.
We begin with the result of
Theorem

3.4
,

\frac{\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n}\overset{p}{\to}1.

In other words,

\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=(1+o_{P}(1))\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n.

Thus, the upper bound in
Theorem

3.3
can be bounded from above as follows

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)({\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+(1+o_{P}(1))\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big(1+(1+o_{P}(1))\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\big)\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n,

Using
\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=O_{P}(1)
again, it follows

\displaystyle\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n.

(31)

A similar argument leads to

\displaystyle\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})(\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}).

(32)

\blacksquare

G.6
Proof of Corollary
3.6

Proof of
Corollary

3.6
.

Under
4
(i) and
5
, we proceed to bound
\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n
in terms of
\trace(\boldsymbol{S})
.
Let
L(\boldsymbol{B})=\frac{1}{2n}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}-\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{B}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lambda\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{2,1}+\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
be the objective function in (
6
), then
L({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})\leq L(\bf 0)
by definition of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lambda\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rVert_{2,1}+\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Now we bound
\frac{1}{n}\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
by Hanson-Wright inequality.
Since
\boldsymbol{Y}=\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{B}^{*}+\boldsymbol{E}
, the rows of
\boldsymbol{Y}
are i.i.d.
{\cal N}_{T}(\bf 0,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
with
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}=(\boldsymbol{B}^{*})^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\boldsymbol{B}^{*}+\boldsymbol{S}
, then
\vec(\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top})\sim{\cal N}(\bf 0,\boldsymbol{I}_{n}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
, and
\boldsymbol{\xi}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}[\boldsymbol{I}_{n}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}]^{-\frac{1}{2}}\vec(\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top})\sim{\cal N}(\boldsymbol{0},\boldsymbol{I}_{nT})
.
Since

\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=[\vec(\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top})]^{\top}\vec(\boldsymbol{Y}^{\top})=\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})\boldsymbol{\xi}
,
we apply the following variant of Hanson-Wright inequality.

Lemma G.1

(Lemma 1 in
[
26
]
)
.

For
\boldsymbol{\xi}\sim{\cal N}(\boldsymbol{0},\boldsymbol{I}_{N})
, then

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{\top}\boldsymbol{A}\boldsymbol{\xi}-\trace(\boldsymbol{A})\leq 2\sqrt{x}\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+2x\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\rVert_{\rm{op}})\geq 1-\exp(-x).

In our case, take
\boldsymbol{A}=(\boldsymbol{I}_{n}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
, then
\trace(\boldsymbol{A})=n\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
,
\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\sqrt{n}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{n}\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
,
\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})
,
thus with probability at least
1-\exp(-x)
,

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-n\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})\leq 2\sqrt{nx}\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})+2x\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}).

Take
x=n
, then with probability at least
1-\exp(-n)
,

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n\leq 5\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}).

Thus,
\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n=O_{P}(1)\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}).
Together with (
31
), we obtain

\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}}).

Note that by
5
,
\trace(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\boldsymbol{y}})=\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\leq(1+\mathfrak{snr})\trace(\boldsymbol{S}).

Therefore, we obtain

\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})\trace(\boldsymbol{S}).

Furthermore, since
\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\leq\sqrt{T}\lVert\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
and
T=o(n)
, we have

\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(\sqrt{T/n})\lVert\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}=o_{P}(1)\lVert\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Finally, since
\lVert\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{*}=\trace(\boldsymbol{S})
, by triangular inequality

\big|\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}\rVert_{*}-\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\big|\leq\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{*}\leq\sqrt{T}\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(\sqrt{T/n})\trace(\boldsymbol{S})=o_{P}(1)\trace(\boldsymbol{S}).

\blacksquare

G.7
Proof of Corollary
3.7

Proof of
Corollary

3.7
.

For
\tau=0
, by the optimality of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}
in (
6
),

\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lambda\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\|_{2,1}\leq\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lambda\|\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\|_{2,1}.

Note that
\boldsymbol{F}=\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})=\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}
, expanding the squares and rearranging terms yields

\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\langle\boldsymbol{E},\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rangle+2n\lambda(\|\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\|_{2,1}-\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\|_{2,1})\leq 2\langle\boldsymbol{E},\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rangle+2n\lambda\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\|_{2,1}.

(33)

From assumptions in this corollary,
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*}
has at most
(1-c)n
rows. Thus, in the event
U_{2}
, we have

n\eta\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=n\eta\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{1/2}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

We bound the right-hand side two terms in (
33
) by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\quad\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\|_{2,1}\leq\sqrt{(1-c)n}\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{(1-c)n}}{\sqrt{\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})}}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{1-c}}{\sqrt{\eta\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}},

and
\langle\boldsymbol{E},\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rangle\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{S}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Therefore, by canceling a factor
\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
from both sides of (
33
), we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\eta}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\lVert\boldsymbol{S}^{\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{S}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}+\frac{2\sqrt{(1-c)}n\lambda}{\sqrt{\eta\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})}}.

Using
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
,

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{4}{n\eta}\trace(\boldsymbol{S})\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{S}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}+\frac{4(1-c)n\lambda^{2}}{\eta^{2}\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})}.

Hence, using
\lambda
is of the form
\mu\sqrt{\trace(\boldsymbol{S})/n}
, we have

\displaystyle\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

(34)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle(1+4\eta^{-1}n^{-1}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{S}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2})\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\frac{4(1-c)\mu^{2}}{\eta^{2}\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})}\trace(\boldsymbol{S})

(35)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle O_{P}(1)(1+\mu^{2})\trace(\boldsymbol{S}),

(36)

where we used that
n^{-1}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}\boldsymbol{S}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=O_{P}(1)
by
[
16
, Theorem II.13]
and
T=o(n)
.
Now, by
Theorem

3.5
,

\displaystyle\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{S}}}-\boldsymbol{S}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})[\trace(\boldsymbol{S})+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}]\leq O_{P}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})(1+\mu^{2})\trace(\boldsymbol{S}),

where the
O_{P}(\cdot)
hides constants depending on
\gamma,c,\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})
since
\eta
is a constant that only depends on
\gamma,c
.

\blacksquare

G.8
Proof of Theorem
B.1

Proof of
Theorem

B.1
.

From the definitions of
\boldsymbol{Q}_{2},\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}
in
Propositions

F.8
and
F.9
, we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}^{\top}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{n^{-2}\big(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}-p\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})}{(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)n^{-\frac{1}{2}}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\big\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)-n^{-2}\big(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}+\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}-p\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\big)\big\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}^{\top}+\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}\rVert_{\rm{F}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)n^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\Theta_{3}(\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n)n^{-\frac{1}{2}},

where
\Theta_{3}=2\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}_{3}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
. The conclusion thus follows by
Propositions

F.8
and
F.9
.

\blacksquare

Appendix H
Proofs of preliminary results

H.1
Proofs of results in Appendix
C

Proof of
Lemma

C.1
.

(i)

For any
\boldsymbol{u}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{T}
, by defintion (
10
),

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{u}

\displaystyle=\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{u}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}_{\hat{{S}}})\big]

\displaystyle\leq\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})[\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}](\boldsymbol{u}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top})\big]

\displaystyle=\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\boldsymbol{u})\otimes[\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}]\big]

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\boldsymbol{u}\right\rVert^{2}\trace[\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\boldsymbol{u}\right\rVert^{2}\trace[\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}].

Let
r=\rank(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\leq\min(n,|\hat{{S}}|)
be the rank of
\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}
, and
\widehat{\phi}_{1}\geq\cdots\geq\widehat{\phi}_{r}>0
be the nonzero eigenvalues of
\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}
. We have

\displaystyle\lVert\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\trace[\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\trace[\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r}{n}\Big\lVert\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\Big\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\widehat{\phi}_{1}}{\widehat{\phi}_{1}+\tau}\leq 1.

Thus,
\lVert\boldsymbol{I}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
as
\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}
is positive semi-definite.

(ii)

Note that

\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=(1-\left\lVert\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\right\rVert_{\rm{op}})^{-1}\leq 1+\frac{\widehat{\phi}_{1}}{\tau},

where

\widehat{\phi}_{1}=\lVert\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq\lVert\frac{1}{n}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq\frac{1}{n}\lVert\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}.

Therefore,

(1)

in the event
\{\lVert\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}<2\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p}\}
, we have

\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1+\tau^{-1}(2+\sqrt{p/n})^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=1+(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}(2+\sqrt{p/n})^{2}.

(2)

{\mathbb{E}}[\widehat{\phi}_{1}]\leq{\mathbb{E}}[n^{-1}\lVert\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}]\leq[(1+\sqrt{p/n})^{2}+n^{-1}]\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}
by (
23
).
Hence,

{\mathbb{E}}\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1+\tau^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}[\widehat{\phi}_{1}]\leq 1+(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}[(1+\sqrt{p/n})^{2}+n^{-1}].

\blacksquare

Proof of
Lemma

C.2
.

(i)

For
\tau=0
, using the same arguement as proof of
Lemma

C.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{u}^{\top}\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{u}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{u}\rVert^{2}\trace[\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}]\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{u}\rVert^{2}|\hat{{S}}|.

Thus, in the event
U_{1}
, we have
\lVert\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}/n\leq|\hat{{S}}|/n\leq(1-c)/2<1
, hence

\lVert\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1.

(ii)

In the event
U_{1}
, we have

\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}=(1-\left\lVert\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n\right\rVert_{\rm{op}})^{-1}\leq(1-(1-c)/2)^{-1}
. Furthermore,
{\mathbb{E}}[I(U_{1})\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}/n)^{-1}\rVert_{\rm{op}}]\leq(1-(1-c)/2)^{-1}.

\blacksquare

Proof of
Lemma

C.3
.

Since
\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\preceq\boldsymbol{M}_{1}^{\dagger}=\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}
,

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{N}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle=\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top})\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top})\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle=\lVert\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq 1,

where the first inequality uses
\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{A}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{A}\boldsymbol{C}\boldsymbol{A}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{op}}
for
0\preceq\boldsymbol{B}\preceq\boldsymbol{C}
.

\blacksquare

H.2
Proofs of results in Appendix
D

Proof of
Lemma

D.1
.

Fixing
\boldsymbol{E}
, if
\boldsymbol{X},\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}
are two design matrices, and
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}},\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}
are the two corresponding multi-task elastic net estimates.
Let
\boldsymbol{Z}=\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}
,

\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}=\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}
,

\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}=\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})
,

\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}=\boldsymbol{Y}-\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}
,
and
\bar{D}=[\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n]^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
Without loss of generality, we assume
\bar{D}\leq D
.
Recall the multi-task elastic net estimate
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}=\mathop{\rm arg\,min}_{\boldsymbol{B}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times T}}\big(\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}-\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+g(\boldsymbol{B})\big)
, where
g(\boldsymbol{B})=\lambda\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{2,1}+\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
Define
\varphi:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}+\boldsymbol{X}(\boldsymbol{B}^{*}-\boldsymbol{B})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+g(\boldsymbol{B})
,

\psi:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

and
\zeta:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\varphi(\boldsymbol{B})-\psi(\boldsymbol{B})
.
When expanding the squares, it is clear that
\zeta
is the sum of a linear function and a
\tau
-strong convex penalty, thus
\zeta
is
\tau
-strongly convex of
\boldsymbol{B}
. Additivity of subdifferentials yields
\partial\varphi({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})=\partial\zeta({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})+\partial\psi({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})=\partial\zeta({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})
. By optimality of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}
we have
{\mathbf{0}}_{p\times T}\in\partial\varphi({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})
, thus
{\mathbf{0}}_{p\times T}\in\partial\zeta({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})
. By strong convexity of
\zeta
,

\zeta(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})-\zeta({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})\geq\langle\partial\zeta({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}),\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rangle+\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which can further be rewritten as

\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+n\tau\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n(g(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})-g({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})),

i.e.,

\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+n\tau\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n(g(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})-g({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})).

Summing the above inequality with its counterpart obtained by replacing
(\boldsymbol{X},{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}},\boldsymbol{H})
with
(\bar{\boldsymbol{X}},\bar{\boldsymbol{B}},\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})
, we have

\displaystyle(LHS)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n\tau^{\prime}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n\tau\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}),\boldsymbol{F}+\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}-\boldsymbol{Z})\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rangle+\langle-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}),\boldsymbol{F}+\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}+(\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}})\boldsymbol{H}\rangle

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\langle(\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}})(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}),\boldsymbol{F}+\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}),(\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}-\boldsymbol{Z})\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rangle+\langle\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}-\boldsymbol{H}),(\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}})\boldsymbol{H}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}})+\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle+\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\Big[\sqrt{\frac{(LHS)}{2n\tau^{\prime}}}(\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}})+\sqrt{(LHS)}(\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}})\Big]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\sqrt{(LHS)}(D+\bar{D})\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}})

where
\tau^{\prime}=\tau\phi_{\min}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{-1})=\tau/\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}
.
That is,

\displaystyle\sqrt{(LHS)}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}2D\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}-\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}=n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert(\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}})\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}[\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}[\sqrt{(LHS)}+\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D]

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D[2\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}})+1].

So far we obtained

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{(LHS)}{2n\tau^{\prime}}}\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}}2\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}-\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D[2\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}})+1].

Let
\boldsymbol{Q}=[\boldsymbol{H}^{\top},\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}/\sqrt{n}]^{\top}
and
\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}=[\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}^{\top},\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}^{\top}/\sqrt{n}]^{\top}
, then
D=\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
,
\bar{D}=\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
. By triangular inequality,

\displaystyle|D-\bar{D}|\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\lVert\boldsymbol{F}-\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}/\sqrt{n}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})],

where the last inequality uses the elementary inequality
\max(a,b)(a+b)\leq 2[\max(a,b)]^{2}
for
a,b>0
with
a=1,b=(2\tau^{\prime})^{-\frac{1}{2}}
.
Let
\frac{\partial D}{\partial\boldsymbol{Z}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\frac{\partial D}{\partial\vec(\boldsymbol{Z})}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1\times np}
, then
\left\lVert\frac{\partial D}{\partial\boldsymbol{Z}}\right\rVert\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}DL_{1}
with
L_{1}=[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})]
. Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{ij}\Big(\frac{\partial D}{\partial z_{ij}}\Big)^{2}=\left\lVert\frac{\partial D}{\partial\boldsymbol{Z}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq n^{-1}D^{2}L_{1}^{2}.

Furthermore, by triangle inequality

\displaystyle\Big\lVert\frac{\boldsymbol{Q}}{D}-\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}{\bar{D}}\Big\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{D}\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\Big|\frac{1}{D}-\frac{1}{\bar{D}}\Big|\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{D}\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\frac{|D-\bar{D}|}{D\bar{D}}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{D}\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\frac{1}{D}\lVert\boldsymbol{Q}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}L,

where
L=8\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})
.
Therefore, when
\tau>0
, we obtain the two mappings
\boldsymbol{Z}\mapsto D^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}/\sqrt{n}
, and
\boldsymbol{Z}\mapsto D^{-1}\boldsymbol{H}
are both
n^{-\frac{1}{2}}L
-lipschitz with
L=8\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})
, where
\tau^{\prime}=\tau/\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}
.

\blacksquare

The proof of
Lemma

D.2
uses a similar argument as proof of
Lemma

D.1
, we present it here for completeness.

Proof of
Lemma

D.2
.

For multi-task group Lasso (
\tau=0
), we restrict our analysis in the event
U_{1}\cap U_{2}
, where
U_{1}=\big\{\lVert{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\rVert_{0}\leq n(1-c)/2\big\}
,
U_{2}=\big\{\inf_{\boldsymbol{b}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p}:\|\boldsymbol{b}\|_{0}\leq(1-c)n}\|\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2}/(n\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2})>\eta\big\}.

Since the only randomness of the problem comes from
\boldsymbol{X}
and
\boldsymbol{E}
, there exists a measurable set
{\cal U}
such that
U_{1}\cap U_{2}=\{(\boldsymbol{X},\boldsymbol{E})\in{\cal U}\}
.
For some noise matrix
\boldsymbol{E}
, consider
\boldsymbol{X},\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}
two design matrices such that
(\boldsymbol{X},\boldsymbol{E})\in{\cal U}
and
(\bar{\boldsymbol{X}},\boldsymbol{E})\in{\cal U}
.
We slightly abuse the notation and let
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}},\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}
denote the two corresponding multi-task group-Lasso estimates.
Thus, the row sparsity of
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}
is at most
n(1-c)
.
Let

\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}=\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*}
,
\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}=\boldsymbol{Y}-\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}
, and
\bar{D}=[\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{F}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n]^{\frac{1}{2}}
.
Without loss of generality, we assume
\bar{D}\leq D
.
Since when
\tau=0
, the multi-task group Lasso estimate is
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}=\mathop{\rm arg\,min}_{\boldsymbol{B}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times T}}\big(\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}-\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+g(\boldsymbol{B})\big)
, where
g(\boldsymbol{B})=\lambda\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{2,1}
.
Define
\varphi:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{E}+\boldsymbol{X}(\boldsymbol{B}^{*}-\boldsymbol{B})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+g(\boldsymbol{B})
,

\psi:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\frac{1}{2n}\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

and
\zeta:\boldsymbol{B}\mapsto\varphi(\boldsymbol{B})-\psi(\boldsymbol{B})
.
Under
\tau=0
, by the same arguments in proof of
D.1
with the same functions
\varphi(\cdot),\psi(\cdot),\zeta(\cdot)
, we obtain

\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n(g(\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})-g({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}})).

Summing the above inequality with its counterpart obtained by replacing
(\boldsymbol{X},{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}},\boldsymbol{H})
with
(\bar{\boldsymbol{X}},\bar{\boldsymbol{B}},\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})
, we have

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Note that in event
U_{1}\cap U_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\eta n\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},\quad\eta n\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{X}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{B}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Thus,

2\eta n\lVert({\widehat{\boldsymbol{H}}}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
, and

\displaystyle(LHS)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\max(2\eta n\lVert\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}(\boldsymbol{H}-\bar{\boldsymbol{H}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\lVert\boldsymbol{E}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\bar{\boldsymbol{H}}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Now, in
U_{1}\cap U_{2}
, the Lipschitz property of the map
\boldsymbol{Z}\mapsto D^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}/\sqrt{n}
follows from the same arguments in proof of
Lemma

D.1
, with
\tau^{\prime}
in
D.1
replaced by
\eta
in this proof.

Furthermore, in the event
U_{1}\cap U_{2}\cap U_{3}
, the Lipschitz property of
\boldsymbol{Z}\mapsto D^{-1}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}/n
follows by triangle inequality. To see this, let
\boldsymbol{U}=D^{-1}\boldsymbol{F}/\sqrt{n}
, and
\boldsymbol{V}=D^{-1}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}/n=n^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{U}
, thus by triangle inequality

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{V}-\bar{\boldsymbol{V}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle=n^{-1/2}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{U}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}^{\top}\bar{\boldsymbol{U}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle=n^{-1/2}[\lVert(\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}})^{\top}\boldsymbol{U}\rVert_{\rm{op}}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}^{\top}(\boldsymbol{U}-\bar{\boldsymbol{U}})\rVert_{\rm{op}}]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1/2}[\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}+\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{U}-\bar{\boldsymbol{U}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}]

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1/2}(1+n^{-1/2}\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}L)\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1/2}(1+(2+\sqrt{p/n})L).

where the last line uses
\lVert\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 2\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p}
in the event
U_{3}
.

\blacksquare

Proof of
Corollary

D.3
.

Corollary

D.3
(1) is a direct consequence of the intermediate result
|D-\bar{D}|\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})]
in proof of
Lemma

D.1
, while
Corollary

D.3
(2) is a direct consequence of the intermediate result
|D-\bar{D}|\leq n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}-\bar{\boldsymbol{Z}}\rVert_{\rm{op}}D[4\max(1,(2\eta)^{-1})]
in proof of
Lemma

D.2
.

\blacksquare

Before proving the derivative formula, we restate
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}
(defined in (
6
) of the full paper) below,

{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}=\mathop{\rm arg\,min}_{\boldsymbol{B}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times T}}\Big(\frac{1}{2n}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Y}-\boldsymbol{X}\boldsymbol{B}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\lambda\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{2,1}+\frac{\tau}{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{B}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\Big),

(37)

where
\|\boldsymbol{B}\|_{2,1}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\|{\boldsymbol{B}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}}\|_{2}
.

For the reader’s convenience, we recall some useful notations.
\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}=\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}
. For each
k\in\hat{{S}}
,
\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}=\lambda\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\|_{2}^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}~\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\|_{2}^{-2}\right)
.
{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}}=\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}).

\boldsymbol{M}_{1}=\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})
,
\boldsymbol{M}=\boldsymbol{M}_{1}+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{pT\times pT}
, and
\boldsymbol{N}=(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top})
.

Proof of
Lemma

D.4
.

We first derive
\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial x_{ij}}
.
Since
\boldsymbol{F}=\boldsymbol{Y}-\boldsymbol{X}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}=\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})
, by product rule,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial x_{ij}}=\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})}{\partial x_{ij}}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}=-\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}(\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})+\boldsymbol{X}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})\boldsymbol{e}_{t},

where

\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{X}}{\partial x_{ij}}=\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}
,
and
\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{\tiny def}}}{{=}}\frac{\partial{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}}{\partial x_{ij}}
.

Now we derive
\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})
from KKT conditions for
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}
defined in (
37
):

1)

For
k\in\hat{{S}}
, i.e.,
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\neq\mathbf{0}
,

\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\big[\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\big]-n\tau\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}=\frac{n\lambda}{\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\|_{2}}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\quad\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1\times T}.

2)

For
k\notin\hat{{S}}
, i.e.,
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}=\mathbf{0}
,

\left\lVert\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\big[\boldsymbol{E}-\boldsymbol{X}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\big]-n\tau\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}\right\rVert<n\lambda.

Here the strict inequality is guaranteed by Proposition 2.3 of
[
4
]
.

Keeping
\boldsymbol{E}
fixed, differentiation of the above display for
k\in\hat{{S}}
w.r.t.
x_{ij}
yields

\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\Big[\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}[\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})+\boldsymbol{X}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}]-n\tau\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\Big]=n\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)},

with
\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}=\lambda\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\|_{2}^{-1}\left(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}~\|{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}{}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\|_{2}^{-2}\right)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{T\times T}
.
Rearranging
and using
\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}=\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}
,

\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\Big[\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\Big]=\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}[(\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}+n\tau\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}+n\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}].

Recall
\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}=\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}
. Multiplying by
\boldsymbol{e}_{k}
to the left and summing over
k\in\hat{{S}}
, we obtain

\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}\Big[\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\Big]=\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}+n\tau\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}+n\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}.

Since
\hat{{S}}
is locally constant in a small neighborhood of
\boldsymbol{X}
,
{\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}_{\hat{{S}}{}^{c}}=0
,
\supp(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})\subseteq\hat{{S}}
. Hence,
\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}=\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}
, and
\boldsymbol{X}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}=\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}
. The above display can be rewritten as

\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}-\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})=(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}+n\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}.

Vectorizing the above display using property
\vec(\boldsymbol{A}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{C})=(\boldsymbol{C}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{A})\vec(\boldsymbol{A})
yields

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}\boldsymbol{e}_{j})\vec(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})-(({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top})\vec(\boldsymbol{e}_{i})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle[\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top})]\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{M}_{1}+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}})\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{M}\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}),

where
\boldsymbol{M}_{1}=\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})
, and
{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}}=\sum_{k\in\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{H}^{(k)}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top})
.

Under
4
(i) that
\tau>0
, it’s obviously that
\rank(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}))=T|\hat{{S}}|
.
Under
4
(ii) that
\tau=0
with
{\mathbb{P}}(U_{1})\to 1
. In the event
U_{1}\cap U_{2}
, we know
\rank(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})=|\hat{{S}}|
from
[
5
, Lemma C.4]
, hence
\rank(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}))=T|\hat{{S}}|
.
In either of the above two scenarios, we thus have
\dim(\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})))=T(p-|\hat{{S}}|)
by rank-nullity theorem. Since
[\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})](\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k})=\bf 0
for
t\in[T],k\in\hat{{S}}^{c}
.
Let
V=\{(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}):t\in[T],k\in\hat{{S}}^{c}\}
be a vector space, then the elements of
V
are linear independent, and
\dim(V)=T(p-|\hat{{S}}|)
. Thus,
V
forms a basis for
\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})
. Since for any
(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k})\in V
, we also have
{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}}(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k})=\bf 0
,
\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}))\subseteq\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}})
. On the other hand, if any vector
\boldsymbol{v}
s.t.
[\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}}]\boldsymbol{v}=\bf 0
, since these matrices are all positive semi-definite, we have
\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{v}=\bf 0
, which implies that
\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}})\subseteq\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}))
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}})

\displaystyle=\ker(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}))

\displaystyle=\mathrm{span}\{(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}):t\in[T],k\in\hat{{S}}^{c}\},

and

\displaystyle\range(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+n\tau\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})+n{\tilde{\boldsymbol{H}}})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{span}\{(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k}):t\in[T],k\in\hat{{S}}\}.

Since
\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}=\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}
,
\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})=(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})
, then
\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})\in\mathrm{col}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})=\range(\boldsymbol{M})
. Since
\boldsymbol{M}
is symmetric,
\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{M}
is the orthogonal projection on the range of
\boldsymbol{M}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})=\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{M}\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})=\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}[(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})-(({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top})]\boldsymbol{e}_{i}.

(38)

Since
\supp(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})\subseteq\hat{{S}}
,
\boldsymbol{X}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}=\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial x_{ij}}

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}(\dot{\boldsymbol{X}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})+\boldsymbol{X}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}})\boldsymbol{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\boldsymbol{e}_{t}+\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}\boldsymbol{e}_{t})

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\boldsymbol{e}_{t}+(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\vec(\dot{\boldsymbol{B}}))

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\boldsymbol{e}_{t}-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}[(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})-(({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top})]\boldsymbol{e}_{i}

\displaystyle=-(e_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})+(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{i})

\displaystyle\quad-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})\boldsymbol{e}_{i}

\displaystyle=-(e_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})+(e_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})

\displaystyle\quad-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})

\displaystyle=-(e_{j}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})

Now we calculate
\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial z_{ij}}
.
Since
\boldsymbol{X}=\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

x_{ik}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}z_{ij}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}
,
\frac{\partial x_{ik}}{\partial z_{ij}}=(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial z_{ij}}=\sum_{k=1}^{p}\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial x_{ik}}\frac{\partial x_{ik}}{\partial z_{ij}}=\sum_{k=1}^{p}\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial x_{ik}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}=D_{ij}^{lt}+\Delta_{ij}^{lt},

where

\displaystyle D_{ij}^{lt}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{k=1}^{p}(\boldsymbol{e}_{k}^{\top}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}({\widehat{\boldsymbol{B}}}-\boldsymbol{B}^{*})\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}),

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{ij}^{lt}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{k=1}^{p}(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\bigl(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p}\bigr)(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{k})(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\bigl(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p}\bigr)(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}}\boldsymbol{e}_{j})

\displaystyle=-(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\bigl(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p}\bigr)(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{j})

It follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}D_{ij}^{it}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}\big[\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\big]\boldsymbol{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}(n\boldsymbol{I}_{T}-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}})\boldsymbol{e}_{t},

where the last line follows from definition of
\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}
in (
10
).

\blacksquare

Proof of
Lemma

D.5
.

(1)

For
\tau>0
, by formula of
\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial z_{ij}}
in
Lemma

D.4
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\rm F}=\sum_{ij}\sum_{lt}\Big(\frac{\partial F_{lt}}{\partial z_{ij}}\Big)^{2}=\sum_{ij}\sum_{lt}\Big(D_{ij}^{lt}+\Delta_{ij}^{lt}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 2\sum_{ij,lt}(D_{ij}^{lt})^{2}+2\sum_{ij,lt}(\Delta_{ij}^{lt})^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{H}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{n})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 2n\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Since
0\preceq\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\preceq\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}
,

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle p\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle p\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{p}{n\tau}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{p}{n\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality uses
\lVert(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq(n\tau)^{-1}
.

On the other hand, we also have

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T\lVert\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\big]\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}+\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\big]\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T(\tau)^{-1}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T\trace\big[(\tau n\boldsymbol{P}_{\hat{{S}}})^{\dagger}\big]\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle T(\tau)^{-1}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{T}{\tau^{\prime}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\rm F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}(T\wedge\frac{p}{n})\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n

\displaystyle\leq 2\max(1,(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}(T\wedge\frac{p}{n}))(\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/n+\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2})

\displaystyle=2\max(1,(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}(T\wedge\frac{p}{n}))D^{2}.

Now by product rule and triangle inequality

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}/D}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 2D^{-2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\frac{\partial D^{-1}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2D^{-2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2D^{-4}\frac{1}{n}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\sum_{ij}\Big(\frac{\partial D}{\partial z_{ij}}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 2D^{-2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2D^{-4}\frac{1}{n}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}n^{-1}D^{2}[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 2D^{-2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{ij}\left\lVert\frac{\partial\boldsymbol{F}}{\partial z_{ij}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2n^{-1}[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle 4\max(1,(\tau^{\prime})^{-1}(T\wedge\frac{p}{n}))+2n^{-1}[4\max(1,(2\tau^{\prime})^{-1})]^{2}

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle f(\tau^{\prime},T,n,p),

where the second inequality is by
Corollary

D.3
.

(2)

For
\tau=0
, by
Lemma

D.2
, in the event
U_{1}\cap U_{2}
, we obtain the same upper bounds as in the first case (1) with
\tau^{\prime}
replaced by
\eta
. To see this,

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\rVert_{\rm{op}}p\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\rVert_{\rm{op}}p\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle p\ \lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\frac{1}{n\eta}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{p}{n\eta}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the third inequality is by
Lemma

C.2
.
Also, we have

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\lVert(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})\rVert_{\rm{op}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{X}_{\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\hat{{S}},\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\big]\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\trace\big[(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\hat{{S}},\hat{{S}}})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\big]\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~
