\displaystyle-\sum_{l}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^{\frac{1}{2}})(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{I}_{p})(\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l})

\displaystyle~=

\displaystyle-\sum_{l}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\bigl(\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\bigr)

\displaystyle~=

\displaystyle-\sum_{l}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X})\boldsymbol{M}^{\dagger}\bigl(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{X}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\bigr)\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle~=

\displaystyle-\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F},

where the last line uses the expression of
\smash{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}}
in (
10
).

It remains to bound the norm of
\boldsymbol{J}_{1}
and
\boldsymbol{J}_{2}
.
To bound
\lVert\boldsymbol{J}_{2}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
, recall the definition of
\boldsymbol{J}_{2}
,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{J}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{ijlt}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}D_{ij}^{lt}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{ijlt}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}(\boldsymbol{e}_{j}^{\top}\boldsymbol{H}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l})\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{ijlt}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{ijl}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{l}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}),

Since
\boldsymbol{N}
is non-negative definite with
\left\lVert\boldsymbol{N}\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
,
\left\lVert\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N}\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\boldsymbol{J}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\otimes\boldsymbol{F})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{l}^{\top})(\boldsymbol{I}_{nT}-\boldsymbol{N})\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\otimes\boldsymbol{F})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\left\lVert(\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\otimes\boldsymbol{F})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{H}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{e}_{l}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{Z}\right\rVert_{\rm{op}}\lVert\boldsymbol{H}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

To bound
\lVert\boldsymbol{J}_{1}\rVert_{\rm{F}}
, recall the definition of
\boldsymbol{J}_{1}
,

\boldsymbol{J}_{1}=\sum_{ij}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\sum_{t}\Delta_{ij}^{it}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}.

For each
t,t^{\prime}\in[T]
,

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{e}_{t^{\prime}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{J}_{1}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{e}_{t^{\prime}}^{\top}\big[\sum_{ij}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\sum_{t}\Delta_{ij}^{it}\boldsymbol{e}_{t}^{\top}\big]\boldsymbol{e}_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\boldsymbol{e}_{t^{\prime}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top}\boldsymbol{Z}\boldsymbol{e}_{j}\Delta_{ij}^{it}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i}(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{t^{\prime}}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{e}_{t}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})

\displaystyle=-\boldsymbol{e}_{t^{\prime}}^{\top}\sum_{i}(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\boldsymbol{e}_{t},

Thus,

\boldsymbol{J}_{1}=\sum_{i}(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i}),

and hence

\displaystyle\lVert\boldsymbol{J}_{1}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\lVert\sum_{i}(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\lVert(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\lVert(\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\otimes\boldsymbol{F}^{\top})\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{N}(\boldsymbol{I}_{T}\otimes\boldsymbol{e}_{i})\rVert_{\rm{op}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i}\lVert\boldsymbol{e}_{i}^{\top}\boldsymbol{F}\rVert\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq n^{\frac{1}{2}}\lVert\boldsymbol{F}\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the first inequality is by sub-additivity of Frobenius norm, the second inequality uses
\lVert\boldsymbol{A}_{1}\boldsymbol{A}_{2}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\lVert\boldsymbol{A}_{1}\rVert_{\rm{F}}\lVert\boldsymbol{A}_{2}\rVert_{\rm{op}}
for any matrices
\boldsymbol{A}_{1},\boldsymbol{A}_{2}
with appropriate dimensions, the third inequality is by
\lVert\boldsymbol{N}\rVert_{\rm{op}}\leq 1
from
Lemma

C.3
, the last inequality is by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

\blacksquare
